###############################################################################
# Plots: blackmail, loose tongues, faction ultimatums, forged claims and
# murder
#
# Was events/plot_events.txt, the vanilla plot events (7040 - 8226). Every
# event has a name now:
#
# Part 1: blackmail                 blackmail.1 - 5 (was 7250 - 7270)
# Part 2: a loose tongue            loosetongue.1 - 4 (was 7040, 7045, 7041,
#                                   7046)
# Part 3: faction ultimatums        ultimatum.N = old id minus 7999 (was
#                                   8000 - 8120 and 8200 - 8226)
# Part 4: cutting a vassal down     cutdown.N = old id minus 7499 (was 7500
#                                   - 7517)
# Part 5: forged claims             fabricate.1 - 3 (was 7600 - 7602)
# Part 6: murder plots              slaying.100 - 436: fourteen ways to kill,
#                                   seventeen events each (see Part 6)
#
# The `break = yes` statements (in the ultimatums and the murder attempts)
# are replaced by a guard flag, plot_step_done on ROOT: set where the break
# was, checked before everything after it, cleared when the option ends.
#
# Callers: decisions/faction_decisions.txt (the faction ultimatums),
#   decisions/plot_decisions.txt (gain a title, gain a vassal title, cut a
#   vassal down, forge a claim), common/cb_types/00_cb_types.txt (the
#   powerful vassal takeover win, ultimatum.227), WHG_vampire_events
#   (a vampire's feeding uses the poisoned wine outcomes, slaying.106 - 116).
#   The opportunity events of Parts 1, 2 and 6 are MTTH events behind the
#   is_part_of_plot pre-trigger, so only plotters and backers are checked.
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_plot_events.csv (the vanilla texts, with the
#   false Pope and "My God" moved into the setting).
###############################################################################

###############################################################################
# Part 1: blackmail (namespace blackmail)
# Faction: Generic
#
# Someone learns of a plot and tries to blackmail the plotter.
#   blackmail.1   The plot leaks (MTTH 20 years, plotters only)
#   blackmail.2   Blackmail, or keep quiet?
#   blackmail.3   The blackmail letter
#   blackmail.4   It worked
#   blackmail.5   It failed
###############################################################################

namespace = blackmail

# blackmail.1 The plot has been leaked
character_event = {
	id = blackmail.1
	desc = EVTDESCblackmail1
	picture = GFX_evt_spymaster
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_rulers = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_plot = plot_gain_title
			has_plot = plot_kill_character
			has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
			has_plot = plot_weaken_fellow_vassal
			has_plot = plot_gain_liege_title
			has_plot = plot_gain_fellow_vassal_title
		}
		is_plot_active = no
		num_of_plot_backers = 1
		any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster NOT = { is_plot_target_of = PREV } }
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				primary_title = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_liege_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_plot_target_of = ROOT }
				any_backed_character = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				employer = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } }
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240

		modifier = { factor = 0.95 liege = { any_plot_backer = { trait = imbecile } } }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			liege = {
				any_plot_backer = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.95 liege = { num_of_plot_backers = 2 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.95 liege = { num_of_plot_backers = 4 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.85 liege = { num_of_plot_backers = 6 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.85 liege = { num_of_plot_backers = 8 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblackmail1
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				limit = {
					primary_title = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_liege_of = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_plot_target_of = ROOT }
					any_backed_character = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					employer = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } }
				}
				letter_event = { id = blackmail.2 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# blackmail.2 Blackmail or not
letter_event = {
	id = blackmail.2
	desc = EVTDESCblackmail2
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblackmail2
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
			modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -25 } } }
		}
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = blackmail.3 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPblackmail3 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBblackmail2
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -50 } } }
		}
	}
}

# blackmail.3 Blackmail attempt
letter_event = {
	id = blackmail.3
	desc = EVTDESCblackmail3

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblackmail3
		wealth = -100
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { letter_event = { id = blackmail.4 days = 1 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBblackmail3
		if = {
			limit = { plot_target_char = { any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } } }
			reveal_plot_w_message = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { NOT = { any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } } }
			}
			reveal_plot = yes
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { letter_event = { id = blackmail.5 days = 1 } } }
	}
}

# blackmail.4 Blackmail succeded
letter_event = {
	id = blackmail.4
	desc = EVTDESCblackmail4

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = "EXCELLENT"
		treasury = 100
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_blackmailed who = FROM months = 60 }
	}
}

# blackmail.5 Blackmail failed
letter_event = {
	id = blackmail.5
	desc = EVTDESCblackmail5

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblackmail5
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 2: a loose tongue (namespace loosetongue)
# Faction: Generic
#
# A drunken backer spills the plot.
#   loosetongue.1 - 2   An ordinary plot revealed (MTTH 20 years)
#   loosetongue.3 - 4   A murder plot revealed (MTTH 20 years)
###############################################################################

namespace = loosetongue

# loosetongue.1 Drunk plot member spills the beans - Non-Murder Plots
character_event = {
	id = loosetongue.1
	desc = EVTDESCloosetongue1
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = {
				has_plot = plot_gain_title
				has_plot = plot_weaken_fellow_vassal
				has_plot = plot_gain_liege_title
				has_plot = plot_gain_fellow_vassal_title
			}
			is_plot_active = no
			OR = { NOT = { religion_group = muslim } trait = hedonist trait = drunkard }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = event7040 }
			NOT = { plot_target_char = { any_known_plotter = { character = PREVPREV } } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = drunkard }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
		modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = genius }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 OR = { trait = quick trait = shrewd } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAloosetongue1
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_plot = plot_gain_title
					has_plot = plot_weaken_fellow_vassal
					has_plot = plot_gain_liege_title
					has_plot = plot_gain_fellow_vassal_title
				}
				NOT = { has_character_flag = event7040 }
				NOT = { plot_target_char = { any_known_plotter = { character = PREVPREV } } }
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = revealed_plot months = 120 }
			set_character_flag = event7040
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = loosetongue.2 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# loosetongue.2 Plot revealed by drunken backer
character_event = {
	id = loosetongue.2
	desc = EVTDESCloosetongue2
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAloosetongue2
		clr_character_flag = event7040
		if = {
			limit = { plot_target_char = { any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } } }
			reveal_plot_w_message = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { NOT = { any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } } }
			}
			reveal_plot = yes
		}
		plot_target_char = {
			opinion = { modifier = scheming_bastard who = ROOT months = 120 }
			if = { #If target is Dwarf and plot is discovered, get Grudged On
				limit = {
					NOT = { dynasty = none }
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_hobgoblin
							trait = creature_skaven
						}
					}
					trait = creature_dwarf
					religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
				}
				set_character_flag = dwarf_grudge_plot_discovered
				character_event = { id = dawi.9 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# loosetongue.3 Drunk plot member spills the beans - Murder Plots
character_event = {
	id = loosetongue.3
	desc = EVTDESCloosetongue1
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			is_plot_active = no
			OR = { NOT = { religion_group = muslim } trait = hedonist trait = drunkard }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = event7041 }
			NOT = { plot_target_char = { any_known_plotter = { character = PREVPREV } } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = drunkard }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
		modifier = { factor = 10.0 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = genius }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 OR = { trait = quick trait = shrewd } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAloosetongue1
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = event7041 }
				NOT = { plot_target_char = { any_known_plotter = { character = PREVPREV } } }
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = revealed_plot months = 120 }
			set_character_flag = event7041
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = loosetongue.4 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# loosetongue.4 Murder plot revealed by drunken backer
character_event = {
	id = loosetongue.4
	desc = EVTDESCloosetongue2
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAloosetongue2

		clr_character_flag = event7041
		if = {
			limit = { plot_target_char = { any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } } }
			reveal_plot_w_message = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { NOT = { any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } } }
			}
			reveal_plot = yes
		}
		plot_target_char = {
			save_event_target_as = target_victim
			ROOT = {
				character_event = { id = crime.2 } # Consequences of discovery
			}
			clear_event_target = target_victim
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 3: faction ultimatums (namespace ultimatum)
# Faction: Diplomacy & realms
#
# A faction leader sends the liege an ultimatum: give a title, change the
# succession, lower crown authority, grant independence, put a claimant on
# the throne, give the council power, depose the ruler, or hand over the
# realm to a powerful vassal. The liege accepts or refuses (war), and the
# backers and the other vassals hear of it.
#   ultimatum.1 - 38      The ultimatums (was 8000 - 8037)
#   ultimatum.51 - 72     Demands accepted (was 8050 - 8071)
#   ultimatum.76 - 100    Backers and vassals informed (was 8075 - 8099)
#   ultimatum.101 - 121   Demands rejected; a plot abandoned (was 8100 -
#                         8120)
#   ultimatum.201 - 205   The antiking (was 8200 - 8204)
#   ultimatum.211 - 215   Overthrow (was 8210 - 8214)
#   ultimatum.216 - 220   Council power (was 8215 - 8219)
#   ultimatum.221 - 227   The powerful vassal takeover (was 8220 - 8226)
###############################################################################

namespace = ultimatum

# ultimatum.1 Ultimatum - gain a title
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.1
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.51 }
				any_plot_backer = { letter_event = { id = ultimatum.71 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { plot_target_title = { usurp_title_plus_barony_if_unlanded = PREV } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { plot_power = 1.5 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { plot_power = 2.0 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { plot_power = 3.0 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { plot_power = 4.0 } }
		}
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.101 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { } }
		}
	}
}

# ultimatum.6 Ultimatum - seniority
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.6
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum6

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_succ_seniority }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_succ_seniority diplomatic_immunity = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 0.5 } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_succ_seniority } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.77 }
			}
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.56 }
				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_succ_seniority
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.76 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			faction_succ_seniority = { # The target (title) of the faction
				succession = seniority
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum6
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 1.5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 1.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 2.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 3.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 1.0 } }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = civilwar.5 days = 120 } # Loyalists flock to the liege's banner
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.106 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_succ_seniority } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 0.75 } }
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = { }
						30 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 0.75 }
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_seniority power = 1.5 } }
					}
					random_list = {
						75 = { }
						5 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ultimatum.11 Ultimatum - primogeniture
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.11
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum11

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_succ_primogeniture }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture diplomatic_immunity = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture power = 0.5 } }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_succ_primogeniture } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.82 }
			}
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.61 }
				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_succ_primogeniture
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.81 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			faction_succ_primogeniture = { # The target (title) of the faction
				succession = primogeniture
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture power = 1.5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture power = 1.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture power = 2.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture power = 3.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_succ_primogeniture power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {

					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }

					add_law_w_cooldown = imperial_crown_authority_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = imperial_crown_authority_3 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = imperial_crown_authority_2
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = imperial_crown_authority_4 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = imperial_crown_authority_3
				}
			}
		}

				if = {

				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = crown_authority_2 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = crown_authority_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = crown_authority_3 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = crown_authority_2
				}

					limit = { has_law = crown_authority_4 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = crown_authority_3
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = imperial_crown_authority_1 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = imperial_crown_authority_0
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = imperial_crown_authority_2 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = imperial_crown_authority_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = imperial_crown_authority_3 }

				}

					limit = { has_law = imperial_crown_authority_4 }

				}
			}
		}

								power = 0.75
							}
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = { }
						30 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_lower_tribal_organization power = 0.75 }
						NOT = {
							faction_power = {
								faction = faction_lower_tribal_organization
								power = 1.5
							}
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						75 = { }
						5 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ultimatum.32 Regent is in on the plot - Tribal Organization

		name = EVTOPTAultimatum27
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {

				any_plot_backer = { letter_event = { id = ultimatum.30 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			crownlaw_title = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = tribal_organization_1 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = tribal_organization_0
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = tribal_organization_2 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = tribal_organization_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = tribal_organization_3 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = tribal_organization_2
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = tribal_organization_4 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = tribal_organization_3
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_lowered_tribal_organization who = ROOT months = 12 }
		}
	}
}

# ultimatum.36 Ultimatum - Independence
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.36
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum36

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_independence }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_independence diplomatic_immunity = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1 # Fold to demand
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 1.0 } } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.001
				FROM = { NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 0.25 } } }
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_independence } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.94 }
			}
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.70 }
				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_independence
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.98 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			any_faction_backer = {
				show_scope_change = no
				faction = faction_independence
				set_defacto_liege = THIS
			}
			set_defacto_liege = THIS
		}

		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_granted_independence who = ROOT months = 120 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1 # War!

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 95
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 1.5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 1.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 2.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 3.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 1.0 } }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = civilwar.5 days = 120 } # Loyalists flock to the liege's banner
				}
			}
		}

		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.120 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_independence } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 0.33 }
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 0.75 } }
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = { }
						15 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						35 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 0.75 }
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_independence power = 1.5 } }
					}
					random_list = {
						75 = { }
						5 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESCultimatum37

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_claimant }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_claimant diplomatic_immunity = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1 # Give in
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_claimant power = 1.0 } } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.001 has_nickname = nick_the_conqueror }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_claimant } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.100 }

				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_claimant
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.99 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.62 }
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1

				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_claimant power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_claimant power = 1.0 } } }

					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = civilwar.5 days = 120 } # Loyalists flock to the liege's banner
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {

			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_claimant } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_claimant power = 0.75 } }
					}

					}

# ultimatum.62 Demand accepted - Install Claimant
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.62
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum62

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		supported_claimant = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_very_grateful who = ROOT years = 10 }
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_fought_for_claimant who = ROOT years = 20 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "EXCELLENT"
		clr_character_flag = faction_claimant_ultimatum_taken
		supported_claimant = {
			if = { limit = { prisoner = yes host = { character = FROM } } prisoner = no }
			ROOT = {
				supported_claimant_title = {
					# If the claimant has the wrong government type, take care of that first.
					if = {
						limit = {
							higher_tier_than = PREVPREV
							holder_scope = {
								OR = { is_feudal = yes is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes }
							}
							ROOT = {
								supported_claimant = {
									is_ruler = yes
									OR = { is_theocracy = yes is_republic = yes }
								}
							}
						}
						ROOT = { supported_claimant = { abdicate = { move = no } } }
					}

					usurp_title_plus_barony_if_unlanded_and_vassals_no_adj = {
						target = PREVPREV
						type = faction_demand
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						NOT = { religion_group = pagan_group }

					}
				}
			}
			add_weak_pressed_claim = FROM
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "GOOD"
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = FROMFROM years = 5 }
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_granted_independence who = FROM months = 120 }
	}

# ultimatum.112 Demand rejected - Install Claimant
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.112
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum101

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		supported_claimant = {
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_fought_for_claimant who = ROOT years = 20 }
		}
		any_faction_backer = {
			faction = faction_claimant
			supported_claimant = {
				reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_fought_for_claimant who = PREV years = 20 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum101

		if = {
			limit = { supported_claimant = { character = ROOT } }
			faction_claimant = { # The target (title) of the faction
				reverse_war = {
					target = ROOT
					casus_belli = claim_on_liege
					faction = faction_claimant
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { supported_claimant = { character = ROOT } } }
			faction_claimant = { # The target (title) of the faction
				reverse_war = {
					target = ROOT
					casus_belli = other_claim_on_liege
					faction = faction_claimant
				}
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = faction_claimant_ultimatum_taken
	}
}

# ultimatum.201 Decadent Debauchery - Notification: Failure, but NOT discovered
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.201
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum201

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_antiking }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_antiking diplomatic_immunity = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 1.0 } } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_antiking } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.203 }
			}
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.204 }
				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_antiking
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.202 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			religion_authority = { modifier = deposed_antipope years = 50 }
			faction_antiking = { # The target (title) of the faction
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = investiture_law_1 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = investiture_law_0
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				religion_head = { opinion = { who = PREV modifier = opinion_deposed_antipope } }
			}
		}

		primary_title = { usurp_title = FROM }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 1.5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 1.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 2.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 3.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 1.0 } } }
				random = {
					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = civilwar.5 days = 120 } # Loyalists flock to the liege's banner
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.205 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_antiking } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 0.75 } }
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = { }
						30 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 0.75 }
						NOT = { faction_power = { faction = faction_antiking power = 1.5 } }
					}
					random_list = {
						75 = { }
						5 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.211
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum211

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_overthrow }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_overthrow diplomatic_immunity = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_overthrow power = 1.0 } } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
		}

		custom_tooltip = { text = khagan_overthrow_execution_warning_tooltip }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_overthrow } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.213 }
			}
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.214 }
				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_overthrow
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.212 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}

		primary_title = { gain_title = { target = FROM type = faction_demand } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_overthrow power = 1.5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_overthrow power = 1.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_overthrow power = 2.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_overthrow power = 3.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_overthrow power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.215 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_overthrow } }
		}
	}

# ultimatum.214 Demand accepted - overthrow
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.214
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum214

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum214
		clr_character_flag = faction_overthrow_ultimatum_taken
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum214
		clr_character_flag = faction_overthrow_ultimatum_taken
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_execution killer = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" }
				FROM = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
						antideath_effect = yes
						ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
						death = { death_reason = death_execution_horses killer = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_increase_council_power
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.217 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_increased_council_power who = ROOT months = 12 } }
		FROM = {
			faction_increase_council_power = { # The target (title) of the faction
				increase_council_power_effect = yes
				increase_vassal_freedom_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 8
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 war = yes }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 1.5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 1.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.35
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 2.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 3.0 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				FROM = { faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 4.0 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 1.0 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = civilwar.5 days = 120 } # Loyalists flock to the liege's banner
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.220 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_increase_council_power } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							faction_power = {
								faction = faction_increase_council_power
								power = 0.75
							}
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = { }
						30 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 0.75 }
						NOT = {
							faction_power = { faction = faction_increase_council_power power = 1.5 }
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						75 = { }
						5 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

# ultimatum.221 Ultimatum - powerful vassal takeover
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.221
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum221

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { in_faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			diplomatic_immunity = yes
			any_faction_backer = {
				faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover
				diplomatic_immunity = yes
			}
		}
		most_popular_vassal = {
			limit = {
				higher_tier_than = BARON
				is_feudal = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable trait = eunuch }
				age = 16
				OR = {
					is_female = no
					AND = { is_woman_trigger = yes ROOT = { has_law = true_cognatic_succession } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = new_title_holder
			save_event_target_as = new_title_holder
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					FROM = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 1.0 }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_weak_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { ai = no NOT = { in_faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover } }
				character_event = { id = ultimatum.223 }
			}
			FROM = {
				letter_event = { id = ultimatum.224 }
				any_faction_backer = {
					faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover
					letter_event = { id = ultimatum.222 }
					diplomatic_immunity = no
				}
				diplomatic_immunity = no
			}
		}
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_abdicated who = ROOT months = 12 } }
		FROM = {
			faction_powerful_vassal_takeover = { # The target (title) of the faction
				holder_scope = {
					any_vassal = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = new_title_holder }
						usurp_title = { target = PREVPREV type = faction_demand }
						clr_character_flag = new_title_holder
						job_chancellor = { remove_title = job_chancellor }
						job_marshal = { remove_title = job_marshal }
						job_treasurer = { remove_title = job_treasurer }
						job_spymaster = { remove_title = job_spymaster }
						job_spiritual = { remove_title = job_spiritual }
					}
				}
				increase_council_power_effect = yes
				increase_vassal_freedom_effect = yes
				increase_vassal_freedom_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { has_law = succession_voting_power_0 }
					add_law_w_cooldown = succession_voting_power_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_law = succ_byzantine_elective } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = overthrow_text
						hidden_tooltip = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									title = e_hre
									NOT = { has_law = succ_hre_elective }
									NOT = { has_law = succ_feudal_elective }
									has_law = law_voting_power_1
									has_law = banish_voting_power_1
									has_law = execution_voting_power_1
									has_law = revoke_title_voting_power_1
									has_law = grant_title_voting_power_1
									has_law = imprison_voting_power_1
									has_law = war_voting_power_1
								}
								add_law_w_cooldown = succ_hre_elective
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									NOT = { title = e_hre }
									NOT = { has_law = succ_hre_elective }
									NOT = { has_law = succ_feudal_elective }
									has_law = law_voting_power_1
									has_law = banish_voting_power_1
									has_law = execution_voting_power_1
									has_law = revoke_title_voting_power_1
									has_law = grant_title_voting_power_1
									has_law = imprison_voting_power_1
									has_law = war_voting_power_1
								}
								add_law_w_cooldown = succ_feudal_elective
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									NOT = { has_law = succ_gavelkind }
									NOT = { has_law = succ_feudal_elective }
									NOT = { has_law = succ_hre_elective }
									NAND = {
										has_law = succ_feudal_elective
										has_law = law_voting_power_1
										has_law = banish_voting_power_1
										has_law = execution_voting_power_1
										has_law = revoke_title_voting_power_1
										has_law = grant_title_voting_power_1
										has_law = imprison_voting_power_1
										has_law = war_voting_power_1
									}
								}
								add_law_w_cooldown = succ_gavelkind
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBultimatum1
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = {
					faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 1.5 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 1.0 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 2.0 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 3.0 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 4.0 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 1.0 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					character_event = { id = civilwar.5 days = 120 } # Loyalists flock to the liege's banner
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			letter_event = { id = ultimatum.225 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPultimatum101 }
			tooltip = { any_faction_backer = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							faction_power = {
								faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover
								power = 0.75
							}
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = { }
						30 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.1 days = 17 } # A great number of rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
						20 = {
							character_event = { id = civilwar.2 days = 17 } # Rebels flock to the revolter's banner
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						faction_power = { faction = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover power = 0.75 }

							}

# ultimatum.222 Inform faction backer - Powerful Vassal Takeover
letter_event = {
	id = ultimatum.222
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum222

	show_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = "GOOD"
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = FROMFROM years = 5 }

		if = {
			limit = { event_target:new_title_holder = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
			add_favor = event_target:new_title_holder
			opinion = { who = event_target:new_title_holder modifier = opinion_favorite years = 5 }
		}

		add_character_modifier = {
			name = faction_powerful_vassal_takeover_ultimatum_timer
			hidden = yes
			duration = 3650
		}
	}
}

# ultimatum.227 Winning war notification - New title holder
character_event = {
	id = ultimatum.227
	desc = EVTDESCultimatum227
	picture = GFX_evt_coronation
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		primary_title = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_law = succession_voting_power_0 }
				add_law_w_cooldown = succession_voting_power_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_law = succ_feudal_elective } }
				add_law_w_cooldown = succ_feudal_elective
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAultimatum227
		clr_character_flag = new_title_holder
		job_chancellor = { remove_title = job_chancellor }
		job_marshal = { remove_title = job_marshal }
		job_treasurer = { remove_title = job_treasurer }
		job_spymaster = { remove_title = job_spymaster }
		job_spiritual = { remove_title = job_spiritual }
		any_vassal = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = helped_win_war }
			character_event = { id = ultimatum.226 days = 1 }
			tooltip = {
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_favorite years = 5 }
				reverse_add_favor = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 4: cutting a rival vassal down to size (namespace cutdown)
# Faction: Diplomacy & realms
#
# A plotter asks the top liege to strip a rival duke of a duchy.
#   cutdown.1 - 3    The top liege is asked
#   cutdown.6 - 12   The duke's ultimatum and its answers
#   cutdown.16, 18   The duchy goes to the duke's vassal, or the plotter
###############################################################################

namespace = cutdown

# cutdown.1 Top Liege is approached by Plotter
letter_event = {
	id = cutdown.1
	desc = EVTDESCcutdown1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Yes
		name = EVTOPTAcutdown1
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.2 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPcutdown2 } }
	}
	option = { #No
		name = EVTOPTBcutdown1
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.3 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPcutdown3 } }
	}
}

# cutdown.2 Plotter informed Top Liege agrees
letter_event = {
	id = cutdown.2
	desc = EVTDESCcutdown2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAcutdown2
		ROOT = { set_character_flag = weaken_vassal_plotter }
		plot_target_char = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.5 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPcutdown5 } }
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = approves_of_my_behaviour who = ROOT years = 2 } }
	}
}

# cutdown.3 Plotter informed Top Liege disagrees
letter_event = {
	id = cutdown.3
	desc = EVTDESCcutdown3

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAcutdown3
		reveal_plot = yes
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = resentful_of_my_behaviour who = ROOT years = 2 } }
	}
}

# cutdown.5 Duke receives ultimatum from Top Liege
letter_event = {
	id = cutdown.5
	desc = EVTDESCcutdown5

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Take it!
		name = EVTOPTAcutdown5
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				plot_target_title = {
					ROOT = {
						any_vassal = {
							tier = count
							any_demesne_title = {
								tier = count
								dejure_liege_title = {
									FROM = { plot_target_title = { title = PREVPREV } }
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			plot_target_title = {
				ROOT = {
					random_vassal = {
						limit = {
							tier = count
							any_demesne_title = {
								tier = count
								dejure_liege_title = {
									FROM = { plot_target_title = { title = PREVPREV } }
								}
							}
						}
						usurp_title = PREVPREV
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { liege = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.9 } } }
	}
	option = { #Never
		name = EVTOPTBcutdown5
		liege = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.10 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPcutdown10 } }
	}
}

# cutdown.6 Duke receives ultimatum from Plotter
letter_event = {
	id = cutdown.6
	desc = EVTDESCcutdown6

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Take it!
		name = EVTOPTAcutdown6
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				plot_target_title = {
					ROOT = {
						any_vassal = {
							tier = count
							any_demesne_title = {
								tier = count
								dejure_liege_title = {
									FROM = { plot_target_title = { title = PREVPREV } }
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			plot_target_title = {
				ROOT = {
					random_vassal = {
						limit = {
							tier = count
							any_demesne_title = {
								tier = count
								dejure_liege_title = {
									FROM = { plot_target_title = { title = PREVPREV } }
								}
							}
						}
						usurp_title = PREVPREV
						hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = cutdown.16 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.7 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCcutdown6
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				FROM = {
					plot_target_title = {
						ROOT = {
							any_vassal = {
								tier = count
								any_demesne_title = {
									tier = count
									dejure_liege_title = {
										FROM = { plot_target_title = { title = PREVPREV } }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# add effect...
		FROM = {
			plot_target_title = { usurp_title = FROM }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = cutdown.18 } }
		}
	}
	option = { #No!
		name = EVTOPTBcutdown6
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = cutdown.8 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPcutdown8 } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 6: murder plots (namespace slaying)
# Faction: Generic
#
# Fourteen ways for a murder plot to strike. Each way has seventeen events,
# at slaying.<base> + 0 - 16:
#   +0   the plotter sees an opportunity (MTTH)
#   +1   a backer sees an opportunity (MTTH)
#   +2 - 4   the backer asks the plotter for funds, and gets them or not
#   +5   the attempt
#   +6 - 9   the target dies or lives, the murderer found out or not
#   +10 - 12 the backer and the plotter agree, the plotter tells the others
#   +13 - 16 notifications to the plotters
# Bases: 100 poisoned wine, 120 a fall (children), 200 archers in ambush,
#   220 a manure explosion, 240 infanticide, 260 a prison break, 280 a
#   bathtub drowning, 300 a boating accident, 320 a poisonous snake, 340 a
#   balcony plummet, 360 a hunting accident, 380 disguised highwaymen, 400
#   a carriage accident, 420 decadent debauchery.
# Fixed: the poisoned wine check meant to spare the faithful of Ormazd (who
#   shun wine) unless decadent, hedonist or a drunkard, but an OR of two
#   NOTs (and a misspelt "ktiabid") let it through for everyone.
###############################################################################

namespace = slaying

# slaying.100 The plotter sees an opportunity - Poisoned Wine
character_event = {
	id = slaying.100
	desc = EVTDESCslaying100
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = {
				NOR = { religion = ormazd religion = kitabid }
				trait = decadent
				trait = hedonist
				trait = drunkard
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying100 # The conspirators provide the poison
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.112 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.105 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying105 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying100 # Buy the poison
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.112 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.105 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying105 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying100 # Buy the poison
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.112 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.105 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying105 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying100 # Hemlock goes well with the Red...
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 learning = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.112 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.105 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying105 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { learning = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.101 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Poisoned Wine
character_event = {
	id = slaying.101
	desc = EVTDESCslaying101
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4200

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 16
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying100 # The conspirators provide the poison
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.110 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.102 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying100 # Buy the poison
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.110 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying100 # Hemlock goes well with the Red...
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { learning = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.110 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.105 Poisoned Wine - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.105
	desc = EVTDESCslaying105
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no

		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying105 # Drinks the wine
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = temperate random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = gluttonous random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = temperate }
						NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.113 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.113 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.106 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.114 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.114 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.107 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying105 # Discovers the poison
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = temperate random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = gluttonous random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = temperate }
					NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.115 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.115 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.108 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.116 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.116 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.109 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.106 Poisoned Wine - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.106
	desc = EVTDESCslaying106
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying106
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_poison killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_poison killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.120 Falling accident - The plotter sees an opportunity
character_event = {
	id = slaying.120
	desc = EVTDESCslaying120
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 5
			NOT = { age = 16 }
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3600

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying120 # The conspirators provide the poison
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.132 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.125 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying125 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying120 # Bribe a Guard
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.132 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.125 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying125 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying120 # Bribe a Guard
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.132 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.125 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying125 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying120 # I know just the person for the job...
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.132 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.125 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying125 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.121 Falling Accident - A plot backer sees an opportunity
character_event = {
	id = slaying.121
	desc = EVTDESCslaying121
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 5
				NOT = { age = 16 }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3600

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 5
					NOT = { age = 16 }
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying120 # The conspirators provide a killer
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.130 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying121 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.122 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying120 # Bribe a Guard
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.130 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying120 # I know just the person for the job...
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.130 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}
