# slaying.125 Falling Accident - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.125
	desc = EVTDESCslaying125
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no

		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying125 # Falls
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } NOT = { trait = trusting } random = 75 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.133 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.133 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.126 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.134 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.134 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.127 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying125 # Dodges
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = { NOT = { trait = trusting } NOT = { trait = paranoid } random = 75 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.135 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.135 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.128 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.136 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.136 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.129 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.126 Falling Accident - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.126
	desc = EVTDESCslaying126
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying126
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.129 Falling Accident - Lives, murderer NOT discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.129
	desc = EVTDESCslaying129
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying129
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } NOT = { trait = trusting } }
			random = { chance = 25 add_trait = paranoid }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = trusting } random = { chance = 50 remove_trait = trusting } }
	}
}

		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying130

		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.131 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying111 } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_plot_backer = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = FROM } }
				character_event = { id = slaying.132 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}

# slaying.200 The plotter sees an opportunity - Archer Ambush
character_event = {
	id = slaying.200
	desc = EVTDESCslaying200
	picture = GFX_evt_longbowman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			is_ruler = yes
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			prisoner = no
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying200 # The conspirators provide the archers
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.205 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying205 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying200 # Hire archers
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { martial = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.205 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying205 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying200 # Hire archers
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { martial = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.205 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying205 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying200 # Knows good archers
		tooltip_info = martial
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 martial = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.205 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying205 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { martial = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.201 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Archer Ambush
character_event = {
	id = slaying.201
	desc = EVTDESCslaying201
	picture = GFX_evt_longbowman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				is_ruler = yes
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				prisoner = no
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 16
					is_ruler = yes
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					prisoner = no
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying200 # The conspirators provide the archers
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.210 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { martial = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.202 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying200 # Hire archers
		trigger = {
			NOT = { martial = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.210 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying200 # Knows good archers
		tooltip_info = martial
		trigger = { martial = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.210 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { martial = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.205 Archer Ambush - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.205
	desc = EVTDESCslaying205
	picture = GFX_evt_longbowman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying205 # Hit by arrows
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = craven random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = craven }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.213 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.213 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.206 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.214 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.214 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.207 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying205 # Avoids arrows
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = craven random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = craven }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.215 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.215 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.208 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.216 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.216 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.209 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.206 Archer Ambush - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.206
	desc = EVTDESCslaying206
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying206
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_arrow killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_arrow killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.220 The plotter sees an opportunity - Manure Explosion
character_event = {
	id = slaying.220
	desc = EVTDESCslaying220
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			prisoner = no
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying220 # The conspirators provide the manure
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.232 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.225 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying225 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying220 # Buy the manure
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { stewardship = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.232 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.225 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying225 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying220 # Buy the manure
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { stewardship = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.232 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.225 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying225 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying220 # Knows farmer
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 stewardship = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.232 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.225 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying225 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { stewardship = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.221 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Manure Explosion
character_event = {
	id = slaying.221
	desc = EVTDESCslaying221
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 16
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying220 # The conspirators provide the manure
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.230 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { stewardship = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.222 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying220 # Buy the manure
		trigger = {
			NOT = { stewardship = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.230 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying220 # Knows farmer
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		trigger = { stewardship = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.230 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { stewardship = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.225 Manure Explosion - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.225
	desc = EVTDESCslaying225
	picture = GFX_evt_council
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying225 # Blown up
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.233 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.233 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.226 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.234 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.234 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.227 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying225 # Not blown up
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.235 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.235 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.228 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.236 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.236 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.229 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.226 Manure Explosion - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.226
	desc = EVTDESCslaying226
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_house
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying226
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.240 The plotter sees an opportunity - Infanticide
character_event = {
	id = slaying.240
	desc = EVTDESCslaying240
	picture = GFX_evt_birth
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { age = 5 }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying240 # The conspirators bribe the maid
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.252 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.245 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying245 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying240 # Bribe maid
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.252 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.245 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying245 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying240 # Bribe maid
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.252 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.245 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying245 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying240 # Knows maid
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.252 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.245 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying245 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.245 Infanticide - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.245
	desc = EVTDESCslaying245
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying245 # Smothered
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = brave random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = gregarious random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = brave }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = gregarious }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.253 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.253 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.246 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.254 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.254 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.247 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying245 # Not smothered
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = brave random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = gregarious random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = gregarious }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					NOT = { trait = brave }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.255 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.255 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.248 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.256 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.256 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.249 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.246 Infanticide - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.246
	desc = EVTDESCslaying246
	picture = GFX_evt_family
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying246
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.260 The plotter sees an opportunity - Prison Break
character_event = {
	id = slaying.260
	desc = EVTDESCslaying260
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 5
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			prisoner = yes
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying260 # The conspirators get the man inside
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.272 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.265 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying265 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying260 # Hire killer
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.272 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.265 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying265 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying260 # Hire killer
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.272 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.265 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying265 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying260 # Knows the man for the job
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.272 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.265 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying265 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.261 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Prison Break
character_event = {
	id = slaying.261
	desc = EVTDESCslaying261
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 5
				prisoner = yes
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 5
					prisoner = yes
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying260 # The conspirators get the man inside
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.270 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.262 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying260 # Hire killer
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.270 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying260 # Knows the man for the job
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.270 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.265 Prison Break - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.265
	desc = EVTDESCslaying265
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying265 # Tries to escape
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = craven random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = craven }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.273 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.273 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.266 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.274 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.274 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.267 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying265 # Does not try to escape
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = craven random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = craven }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.275 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.275 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.268 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.276 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.276 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.269 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.266 Prison Break - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.266
	desc = EVTDESCslaying266
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying266
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.280 The plotter sees an opportunity - Bathtub Drowning
character_event = {
	id = slaying.280
	desc = EVTDESCslaying280
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 5
			trait = incapable
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying280 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.292 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.285 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying285 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying280 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.292 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.285 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying285 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying280 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.292 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.285 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying285 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying280 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.292 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.285 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying285 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.281 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Bathtub Drowning
character_event = {
	id = slaying.281
	desc = EVTDESCslaying281
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 5
				trait = incapable
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				prisoner = no
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 5
					trait = incapable
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					prisoner = no
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying280 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.290 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.282 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying280 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plotter = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.290 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying280 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.290 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

	id = slaying.285
	desc = EVTDESCslaying285
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying285 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { is_strong_trigger = yes random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
					AND = { is_weak_trigger = yes random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						is_weak_trigger = no
						is_strong_trigger = no
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.293 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.293 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.286 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.294 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.294 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.287 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying285 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { is_strong_trigger = yes random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
				AND = { is_weak_trigger = yes random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					is_weak_trigger = no
					is_strong_trigger = no
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.295 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.295 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.288 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.296 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.296 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.289 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.286 Bathtub Drowning - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.286
	desc = EVTDESCslaying286
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying286
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_drowning killer = FROMFROMFROM }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_drowning killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.287 Bathtub Drowning - Dies, murderer NOT discovered

# slaying.300 The plotter sees an opportunity - Boating Accident
character_event = {
	id = slaying.300
	desc = EVTDESCslaying300
	picture = GFX_evt_kraken
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 5
			NOT = { age = 16 }
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3600

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying300 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.312 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.305 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying305 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying300 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.312 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.305 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying305 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying300 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.312 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.305 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying305 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying300 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 learning = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.312 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.305 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying305 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { learning = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.301 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Boating Accident
character_event = {
	id = slaying.301
	desc = EVTDESCslaying301
	picture = GFX_evt_kraken
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 5
				NOT = { age = 16 }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				prisoner = no
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3600

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 5
					NOT = { age = 16 }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					prisoner = no
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying300 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.310 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.302 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying300 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.310 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying300 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { learning = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.310 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.305 Boating Accident - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.305
	desc = EVTDESCslaying305
	picture = GFX_evt_kraken
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying305 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.313 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.313 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.306 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.314 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.314 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.307 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying305 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { is_strong_trigger = yes random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
				AND = { is_weak_trigger = yes random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					is_weak_trigger = no
					is_strong_trigger = no
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.315 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.315 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.308 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.316 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.316 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.309 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.306 Boating Accident - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.306
	desc = EVTDESCslaying306
	picture = GFX_evt_kraken
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying306
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_drowning killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_drowning killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}
