# slaying.320 The plotter sees an opportunity - Poisonous Snake
character_event = {
	id = slaying.320
	desc = EVTDESCslaying320
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying320 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.332 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.325 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying325 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying320 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.332 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.325 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying325 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying320 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.332 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.325 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying325 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying320 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 learning = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.332 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.325 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying325 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { learning = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.321 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Poisonous Snake
character_event = {
	id = slaying.321
	desc = EVTDESCslaying321
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 16
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying320 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.330 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.322 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying320 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.330 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying320 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { learning = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.330 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying104 # Pay for it myself
		scaled_wealth = -1.0
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.325 days = 27 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying325 }
		}

	option = {

		FROM = { clr_character_flag = murder_in_motion }

# slaying.325 Poisonous Snake - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.325
	desc = EVTDESCslaying325
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying325 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = craven random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = slothful random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = craven }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = slothful }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.333 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.333 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.326 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.334 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.334 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.327 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying325 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = craven random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = slothful random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = craven }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = slothful }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.335 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.335 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.328 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.336 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.336 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.329 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.326 Poisonous Snake - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.326
	desc = EVTDESCslaying326
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying326
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_snakes killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_snakes killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.335 Poisonous Snake - Notification: Failure, and discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.335
	desc = EVTDESCslaying335
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# Set opinions vs the bungling killer
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_murder_attempt months = 120 } }
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_dishonorable months = 60 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { FROM = { employer = { character = ROOT } } } }
			FROM = {
				employer = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_dishonorable months = 60 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			any_dynasty_member = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_dishonorable months = 60 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { character = FROM }
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			}
			reveal_plot = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying335
		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { character = FROM }
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			}
			clr_character_flag = murder_in_motion
			tooltip = { reveal_plot = yes }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.340 The plotter sees an opportunity - Balcony Plummet
character_event = {
	id = slaying.340
	desc = EVTDESCslaying340
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying340 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.352 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.345 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying345 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying340 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.352 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.345 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying345 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying340 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.352 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.345 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying345 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying340 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.352 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.345 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying345 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.341 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Balcony Plummet
character_event = {
	id = slaying.341
	desc = EVTDESCslaying341
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 16
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying340 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.350 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.342 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying340 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.350 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying340 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.350 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.342 Balcony Plummet - Plot backer requests funds
character_event = {
	id = slaying.342
	desc = EVTDESCslaying342
	picture = GFX_evt_spymaster
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying342
		transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = FROM value = 1.0 }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.343 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying103 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying102
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.344 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying104 } }
	}
}

# slaying.345 Balcony Plummet - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.345
	desc = EVTDESCslaying345
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying345 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.353 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.353 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.346 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.354 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.354 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.347 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying345 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.355 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.355 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.348 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.356 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.356 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.349 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.346 Balcony Plummet - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.346
	desc = EVTDESCslaying346
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying346
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.360 The plotter sees an opportunity - Hunting Accident
character_event = {
	id = slaying.360
	desc = EVTDESCslaying360
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 5
			NOT = { age = 16 }
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3600

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying360 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.372 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.365 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying365 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying360 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.372 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.365 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying365 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying360 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.372 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.365 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying365 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying360 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.372 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.365 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying365 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.361 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Hunting Accident
character_event = {
	id = slaying.361
	desc = EVTDESCslaying361
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 5
				NOT = { age = 16 }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3600

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5
				plot_target_char = {
					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
					age = 5
					NOT = { age = 16 }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying360 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.370 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.362 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying360 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.370 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying360 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue

			character_event = { id = slaying.370 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no

# slaying.365 Hunting Accident - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.365
	desc = EVTDESCslaying365
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying365 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = gregarious random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = gregarious }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.373 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.373 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.366 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.374 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.374 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.367 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying365 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = gregarious random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = gregarious }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.375 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.375 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.368 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.376 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.376 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.369 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.366 Hunting Accident - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.366
	desc = EVTDESCslaying366
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying366
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_arrow killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_arrow killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.380 The plotter sees an opportunity - Disguised Highwaymen
character_event = {
	id = slaying.380
	desc = EVTDESCslaying380
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying380 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.392 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.385 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying385 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying380 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.392 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.385 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying385 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying380 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.392 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.385 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying385 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying380 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.392 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.385 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying385 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.381 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Disguised Highwaymen
character_event = {
	id = slaying.381
	desc = EVTDESCslaying381
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}

				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}

		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = {

			}

			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }

			}

			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0

		name = EVTOPTAslaying380 # The conspirators bribe servants

		}

	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying380 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.390 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.385 Disguised Highwaymen - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.385
	desc = EVTDESCslaying385
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying385 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.393 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.393 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.386 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.394 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.394 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.387 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying385 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.395 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.395 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.388 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.396 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.396 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.389 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.386 Disguised Highwaymen - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.386
	desc = EVTDESCslaying386
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying386
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.400 The plotter sees an opportunity - Carriage Accident
character_event = {
	id = slaying.400
	desc = EVTDESCslaying400
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying400 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.412 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.405 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying405 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying400 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.412 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.405 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying405 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying400 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.412 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.405 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying405 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying400 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.412 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.405 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying405 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.401 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Carriage Accident
character_event = {
	id = slaying.401
	desc = EVTDESCslaying401
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = {
				NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
				had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
			}
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8400

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}

			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 15 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 0.5

					NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }

					NOT = { trait = incapable }

					had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
				}
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = plot_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying400 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.410 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.402 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying400 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.410 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying400 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.400 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.405 Carriage Accident - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.405
	desc = EVTDESCslaying405
	picture = GFX_evt_carriage
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying405 # Drowns
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.413 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.413 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.406 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.414 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.414 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.407 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying405 # Does not drown
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = diligent random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.415 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.415 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.408 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.416 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.416 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.409 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.406 Carriage Accident - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.406
	desc = EVTDESCslaying406
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying406
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_fall killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.420 The plotter sees an opportunity - Decadent Debauchery
character_event = {
	id = slaying.420
	desc = EVTDESCslaying420
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			NOT = { is_murder_quest_target_of_root_trigger = yes }
			age = 16
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			trait = decadent
			dynasty = ROOT
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
			had_character_flag = { flag = murder_in_motion days = 100 }
		}
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 7300

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 1.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_power = 2.0
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying420 # The conspirators bribe the servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.432 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.425 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying425 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying420 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.432 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.425 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying425 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying420 # Bribe servants
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.432 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.425 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying425 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying420 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 intrigue = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = slaying.432 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		event_target:plot_target = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.425 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying425 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { intrigue = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# slaying.421 A plot backer sees an opportunity - Decadent Debauchery
character_event = {

	desc = EVTDESCslaying421

				age = 16

				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }

			}

			}
		}

			any_backed_character = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }

			any_backed_character = {

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying420 # The conspirators bribe servants
		trigger = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.430 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying101 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.422 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying102 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying420 # Bribe servants
		trigger = {
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.430 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaying420 # Replace with own servants
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { intrigue = 10 }

		event_target:plot_leader = {
			character_event = { id = slaying.420 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPslaying110 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaying100 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { intrigue = 10 }
			NOT = { event_target:plot_leader = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# slaying.425 Decadent Debauchery - the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = slaying.425
	desc = EVTDESCslaying425
	picture = GFX_evt_jugglers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	trigger = {
		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying425 # Dies
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = slothful random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = proud random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = hedonist random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = gluttonous random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = drunkard random = 90 }
				AND = { trait = lustful random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = drunkard }
					NOT = { trait = hedonist }
					NOT = { trait = lustful }
					NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
					NOT = { trait = slothful }
					NOT = { trait = proud }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.433 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.433 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.426 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.434 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.434 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.427 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaying425 # Does not die
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = slothful random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = proud random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = hedonist random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = gluttonous random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = drunkard random = 90 }
					AND = { trait = lustful random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = drunkard }
						NOT = { trait = hedonist }
						NOT = { trait = lustful }
						NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
						NOT = { trait = slothful }
						NOT = { trait = proud }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.435 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.435 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.428 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.436 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaying.436 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = slaying.429 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaying.426 Decadent Debauchery - Dies, murderer(s) discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.426
	desc = EVTDESCslaying426
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying426
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROMFROM }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROMFROM }
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

# slaying.427 Decadent Debauchery - Dies, murderer NOT discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.427
	desc = EVTDESCslaying426
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying427

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
					plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
				antideath_effect = yes
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROMFROMFROM }
				ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
					antideath_effect = yes
					ROOT = { set_character_flag = plot_step_done }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_character_flag = plot_step_done } } }
					death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = { clr_character_flag = plot_step_done }
	}
}

		}
	}

# slaying.435 Decadent Debauchery - Notification: Failure, and discovered
character_event = {
	id = slaying.435
	desc = EVTDESCslaying435
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = crime.2 } # Consequences of discovery
		clear_event_target = target_victim

		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { character = FROM }
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			}
			reveal_plot = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaying435
		if = {
			limit = {
				plot_target_char = { character = FROM }
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
			}
			clr_character_flag = murder_in_motion
			tooltip = { reveal_plot = yes }
		}
	}
}
